Result: 对于分段常数效用，算法为精确算法，运行时间为伪多项式时间。对于分段利普希茨效用，算法同样为伪多项式时间，并能产生ε-最优策略。复杂性方面，即使效用函数仅为分段常数，所研究的策略综合问题也已是PSPACE-困难。

Conclusion: 分类器自动机以线性状态复杂度替代比较器交叉积带来的指数膨胀，为具有不连续或分段规则效用的折扣回报优化提供了可计算的自动机基础；但问题的PSPACE困难性表明，其最坏情形计算难度仍然较高。

Abstract: Categorizing continuous data into discrete bins is a fundamental operation in artificial intelligence. We introduce the categorizer automaton, a deterministic automaton that reads an infinite sequence of rewards and identifies which of finitely many bins contains its discounted sum. Categorizer automata generalize comparator automata, the special case of two bins, which have already proven useful in quantitative synthesis. Our main technical contribution is the construction of a categorizer automaton whose state space is linear in the number of bins, rather than exponential as obtained by a cross-product of comparator automata. We then apply categorizer automata to Markov decision processes, where they allow one to synthesize policies that maximize the expected utility of a discounted-sum payoff for utility functions that may be discontinuous. For piecewise-constant utility functions, the resulting algorithm is exact and runs in pseudo-polynomial time. For piecewise-Lipschitz utility functions, a class that includes any utility with bounded slope between finitely many jumps, it again runs in pseudo-polynomial time and yields an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy. We also show that the synthesis problem considered is PSPACE-hard already for piecewise-constant utilities.

</details>


### [66] [AI Control Scientist: LLM-driven Agentic System for Automated Control Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26780)
*Haiteng Wang,Weihao Li,Jing Zhang,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Control Scientist（AICS），一种由大语言模型驱动的智能体系统，可根据自然语言设计需求自动生成并优化控制器。系统通过任务建模、控制器设计和参数调节三个智能体协同完成控制系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制系统设计高度依赖专家经验和人工参数整定，效率低、可扩展性有限，难以满足现代工业中复杂控制任务的自动化需求。因此，研究旨在将自然语言需求转化为工程约束，并实现控制器结构、代码和参数的自动生成与优化。

Method: AICS由三个核心模块组成：任务建模智能体负责解析用户需求并提取工程约束；控制器设计智能体负责生成候选控制器结构及可执行代码；参数调节智能体在闭环性能指标下优化控制器参数。整体流程以闭环仿真或性能评估结果作为反馈，迭代改进控制方案。

Result: 实验表明，AICS能够自动生成多种具有代表性的控制系统，并且在设计成功率和优化效率方面优于现有自动化基线方法。摘要未提供具体实验任务、数据规模、性能数值或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 研究证明，大语言模型驱动的智能体系统具备自动化控制器设计与优化的可行性，有望降低控制系统开发对人工经验的依赖，并推动模型预测控制等高级控制方法的自动化设计。不过，其工业部署能力仍需通过真实系统验证、稳定性与安全性分析以及跨任务泛化实验进一步确认。

Abstract: Control system design is critical for modern industry, such as chemical process temperature regulation and aero-engine control. However,traditional control design workflows rely heavily on expert knowledge and extensive manual parameter tuning, resulting in limited efficiency and scalability. To this end, this paper proposes AI Control Scientist (AICS), the first large language model (LLM)-driven agent capable of automatically generating optimized controller from language design requirements. Specifically, a Task Modeling Agent interprets user requirements to engineering constraints; a Controller Design Agent generate candidate controller structures and executable code; and a Parameter Tuning Agent refine controller parameters under closed-loop performance criteria. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed agentic system can automatically generate multiple representative control systems, outperforms existing automated baselines in both design success rate and optimization efficiency. This work has the potential to transform control system design from human-driven to agent-driven, paving the way for model predictive control and other advanced control systems design.

</details>


### [67] [Decoupling Planning and Control for Instructable Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26788)
*Zineng Tang,Kelsey R. Allen,Sjoerd van Steenkiste,Ishita Dasgupta,Alane Suhr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Instruct-to-Act将高层视觉语言模型（VLM）规划器与高频世界模型控制器解耦结合：VLM生成稀疏文本指令，控制器据此在陌生环境中快速执行动作。该方法在七个具身环境中整体优于仅控制器和直接VLM动作生成方案，并保持低延迟控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练且经过指令微调的VLM擅长根据指令和观测生成高层计划，但难以将计划稳定地转化为低延迟动作序列；世界模型控制器能够进行快速观测到动作控制，却缺乏开放式任务指导。因此，研究目标是结合VLM的任务规划能力与世界模型控制器的高频执行能力。

Method: 系统采用分层、解耦架构：VLM规划器以较低频率生成稀疏的高层文本指令，世界模型控制器在高频率下自主执行。为使控制器能够理解语言指令，研究者对控制器策略轨迹片段进行合成指令重标注，并联合优化行为克隆损失、既有的奖励最大化目标和世界模型学习目标。该系统还支持直接替换不同的预训练VLM规划器，无需重新微调。

Result: 在七个具身环境中进行评估，其中包括三个需要多智能体通过语言协作的环境。相比匹配观测空间和动作空间下的仅控制器方法及直接VLM动作生成方法，Instruct-to-Act表现 consistently 更优，同时保持快速控制能力。在七项任务中的六项任务上，该方法与强大的视觉语言动作模型和多智能体强化学习基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 将高层语言规划与低层世界模型控制分离并协同训练，能够有效弥合VLM规划与可靠实时执行之间的差距。该方法兼具开放式任务指导、低延迟动作控制、跨规划器可替换性和多智能体语言协作能力，但仍有一项任务未达到强基线水平，表明其泛化能力和任务适应性仍存在改进空间。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pre-trained, instruction-tuned vision-language models (VLMs) perform well at mapping from instructions and observations to high-level plans, but struggle to realize such plans as reliable low-latency action sequences in unfamiliar environments. At the same time, world-model controllers excel at fast observation-to-action control, but lack open-ended task guidance. In this work, we combine these strengths into a single system, Instruct-to-Act, where we train a world-model controller to act autonomously at high frequency when conditioned on sparse, higher-latency, and high-level text instructions generated by a VLM planner. To train controllers to be language-instructable, we relabel segments of controller policy rollouts with synthetic instructions and jointly optimize a behavior-cloning objective along with existing reward-maximizing and world-modeling objectives. We evaluate our proposed approach across seven embodied environments, including three multi-agent environments where VLM planners coordinate through language while trained controllers serve as their actuators. Under matched observation and action spaces, our decoupled approach consistently outperforms controller-only and direct VLM action-generation variants, preserves fast control, and lets us swap in different pretrained VLM planners without fine-tuning, while remaining competitive with strong vision-language-action and multi-agent RL baselines on six of seven tasks.

</details>


### [68] [SymbolLKG: Towards Verifiable Logical Reasoning via Logical Knowledge Graph and Symbolic Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26836)
*Haizhao Fan,Yuchi Xiong,Jize Wang,Xinping Guan,Xinyi Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号架构，将逻辑知识图谱（LKG）、动态逻辑求解器路由和拓扑感知混合检索结合起来，以提升大语言模型在严格多步逻辑推理中的准确性、一致性和可验证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然具备较强的自然语言理解能力，但在严格的多步推理任务中容易产生幻觉、推理不一致和不可验证结论。链式思维缺乏系统验证机制，传统检索增强生成又难以表达逻辑任务中的复杂结构依赖，因此需要显式建模逻辑规则、约束及其依赖关系。

Method: 方法包括三部分：一是构建基于本体的逻辑知识图谱，将逻辑规则和约束作为一等拓扑节点，并从文本中抽取和组织依赖关系；二是设计Logic Router，根据任务特征将推理任务动态分派给最适合的符号推理引擎；三是引入拓扑感知的混合检索机制，为路由和推理过程提供结构化支持，从而形成神经模型与符号求解器协同的推理流程。

Result: 在逻辑推理基准测试中，该框架显著优于现有提示方法和传统RAG基线，在准确率方面取得更好表现，并能够生成可验证的推理路径。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标提升幅度、统计显著性或消融实验结果。

Conclusion: 将逻辑结构显式编码到知识图谱中，并结合动态求解器选择与结构感知检索，有望缓解大语言模型在复杂多步逻辑推理中的幻觉和不一致问题。该工作证明了神经符号协同路线的有效性，但其泛化能力、额外计算开销以及不同符号引擎之间的路由可靠性仍需通过更详细实验验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in natural language understanding, yet they struggle with strict multi-step reasoning, frequently suffering from hallucinations and inconsistency. Existing solutions like Chain-of-Thought (CoT) lack rigorous verification mechanisms, while standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) often misses the complex, structural dependencies inherent in logical tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose a Neuro-Symbolic architecture that integrates a Logical Knowledge Graph (LKG) with dynamic solver routing. Specifically, we introduce an ontology-based LKG that treats logical rules and constraints as first-class topological nodes, enabling explicit modeling of dependencies extracted from text. We further design a Logic Router to dynamically dispatch tasks to the optimal symbolic engine, which is supported by a topology-aware hybrid retrieval mechanism. Experimental results on logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prompting and RAG baselines, delivering higher accuracy and verifiable reasoning paths.

</details>


### [69] [LiveSim: Simulating Environment-Shaped Users in Multi-Agent Live-Stream Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26849)
*Jiaqi Xu,Yiran Qiao,Jing Chen,Qiwei Zhong,Xiang Ao,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiveSim是一种面向直播生态模拟的基于大语言模型的用户行为模拟框架，通过动态修正用户行为假设并积累可迁移的环境—行为模式，提升个体行为拟合度，并支持生态层面的风险演化与平台干预分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体生态模拟器主要依赖从历史数据中推断的静态用户画像，难以刻画直播等高社交强度环境中由持续互动和环境因素引起的行为变化，因此需要能够随交互轨迹动态更新用户行为模型的方法。

Method: LiveSim将用户表示为可编辑的行为假设，并利用基于行为轨迹的交互过程持续进行修正。当模拟轨迹与观测轨迹存在差异时，框架将差异视为潜在的环境塑造效应，进一步提取可迁移的环境—行为模式，并将其累积到集体行为记忆中，以同时增强用户级行为保真度和生态级模拟能力。

Result: 基于真实直播风险控制数据的实验表明，LiveSim能够提升用户级行为模拟的保真度，并支持对生态系统中风险演化过程及平台干预效果的分析。摘要未报告具体数据集规模、评价指标或相对基线的定量提升幅度。

Conclusion: 动态行为假设更新、轨迹差异归因和集体行为记忆相结合，可以弥补静态用户画像在社会化环境模拟中的不足，使大语言模型更适合用于直播生态中的用户行为建模与系统级分析。

Abstract: User behavior simulation with large language models~(LLMs) is increasingly used to support multi-agent ecosystem simulation. Existing simulators typically rely on static user profiles inferred from historical observations, which become inadequate in socially intensive environments such as live streaming where interaction dynamics continuously reshape user behavior. We propose \textbf{LiveSim}, an LLM-based framework for live-stream ecosystem simulation. It represents users as editable behavioral hypotheses and progressively refines them through trajectory-grounded interactions, where discrepancies between simulated and observed trajectories reveal missing environmental shaping effects. These signals are further extracted as transferable environment-behavior patterns and accumulated in a collective behavioral memory to improve user-level behavioral fidelity and support ecosystem-level simulation. Experiments on real-world live-stream risk-control data validate the effectiveness of LiveSim in improving user-level behavioral fidelity and enabling ecosystem-level analysis of risk evolution and platform intervention effects.

</details>


### [70] [BekchiAI: Measuring, Observing, and Controlling LLM Agents in One Click](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26867)
*Mesut Toruk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BekchiAI提出一个可验证的智能体评测基准与观测控制平台，用于衡量工具调用、规划、依赖处理、输入判断和论证 grounding 等超越准确率的智能体能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仅依赖准确率的排行榜难以反映多步工具使用智能体在工具顺序、依赖规划、不可信输入判断和证据支撑方面的真实表现，因此需要可复现、可验证且包含行为指标的综合评测体系。

Method: 构建BekchiAI-Benchmark，包含13个基于ReAct的工具使用智能体，覆盖算术、结构化查询/SQL、安全检测、URL grounding、规划、编排和工具策略7类任务，共2057个确定性测试任务。任务答案通过真实数据库中的规范SQL、DAG精确调度结果或封闭形式函数进行验证，并加入配对的对抗性安全样本和不完美签名扫描器，以避免模型简单复制外部判定。评测除准确率外，还统计工具调用遵循度、URL幻觉率、来源匹配度及模型级token成本；同时开发Web平台，提供完整token与延迟遥测以及远程终止运行功能。

Result: 研究对Qwen3.7-Max、gemma-4-31B-it、gemma4:26b和gpt-oss-120b进行了比较，结果显示不同模型在各任务类别上的表现差异比总体聚合分数更具信息量。基准、评测脚本和平台均公开发布。

Conclusion: BekchiAI将可验证任务、细粒度行为指标和部署期可观测性结合起来，为评估及管理多步工具使用智能体提供了更全面的基础；其主要价值在于揭示模型在具体智能体技能维度上的差异，而非仅给出单一准确率排名。

Abstract: Large language model agents reason, call tools, and act autonomously over many steps, but their agentic skills-correctly sequencing tools, planning under dependencies, judging untrusted inputs, and grounding generated arguments-are hard to measure with accuracy-only leaderboards. We present BekchiAI, which addresses both sides: a benchmark for measuring agentic skill and a platform for observing and controlling live agents. The BekchiAI-Benchmark, a suite of 13 tool-using ReAct agents across 7 task categories (arithmetic, structured/SQL, security detection, URL grounding, planning, orchestration, and tool-policy), totalling 2,057 deterministic, committed test tasks. Every task is verifier-checkable gold answers are computed by running canonical SQL against a real database, computing the exact schedule of a directed acyclic graph (DAG), or evaluating closed-form lambdas including adversarial security samples paired with deliberately imperfect signature scanners so a score reflects the model's own judgment, not the copying of an oracle. We define a small set of behavioral metrics beyond accuracy-tool-call adherence, URL hallucination and source-match, and per-model token cost and report a four-model comparison (Qwen3.7-Max, gemma-4-31B-it, gemma4:26b, gpt-oss-120b) whose story is in the per-family spread, not the aggregate. The benchmark runs are executed using the provided evaluation scripts. BekchiAI-Platform is a complementary web-based observability and control layer for deployed agents, providing full token and latency telemetry as well as remote run termination. The benchmark, evaluation tools, and platform are publicly released.

</details>


### [71] [C-Unseen: Weak Signal Detection in Dynamic Temporal Knowledge Graphs via LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26870)
*Yassir Lairgi,Ludovic Moncla,Khalid Benabdeslem,Rémy Cazabet,Pierre Cléau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: C-Unseen是一种面向动态时序知识图谱的可解释弱信号检测框架，通过识别与主流叙事存在张力且跨时间持续扩散的稀有语义子图，实现弱信号发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于关键词频率、主题模型或无类型图拓扑的方法难以刻画弱信号中的语义关联结构与关系演化，容易遗漏低可见度但具有潜在重要性的早期变化迹象。

Method: 框架包含两个模块：稀有子图提取器利用大语言模型和链式推理，识别内容上偏离当前快照主流叙事的稀有且语义连贯的子图；弱信号告警器跨连续时序知识图谱快照跟踪这些子图的持续性与扩散情况，从而筛选真实弱信号。

Result: 实验结果表明，C-Unseen在弱信号检测任务上优于基于关键词、主题和图结构的基线方法。

Conclusion: 弱信号可被建模为在连续时序快照中扩散的稀有、语义连贯子图。结合大语言模型语义解释能力与跨时间持久性分析，C-Unseen能够提升检测效果并提供自解释结果。

Abstract: Weak signals are early, low-visibility indicators that precede significant changes before those changes become established. Existing detection methods, based on keyword frequency, topic modeling, or untyped graph topology, fail to capture the semantic and relational structure through which such signals manifest. In this paper, we propose C-Unseen, a self-interpretable framework for weak signal detection in Dynamic Temporal Knowledge Graphs (DTKGs). We define a weak signal as a rare, semantically coherent subgraph that proliferates across consecutive TKG snapshots. The framework operates through two modules: a Rare Subgraphs Extractor, in which an LLM identifies subgraphs whose content is in tension with the dominant snapshot narrative via chain-of-thought reasoning, and a Weak Signal Alerter, in which the persistence of these rare subgraphs is tracked across time steps to isolate true weak signals. Experimental results demonstrate that C-Unseen outperforms keyword-, topic-, and graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [72] [Learning-Augmented Online Allocation under Unreliable Advice: Robustness, Exposure Fairness, and Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26889)
*Fredy Pokou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究带预测信息的在线分配问题，提出兼顾效率、鲁棒性与公平性的决策规则，在预测可靠时利用其提升性能，在预测失误或具有对抗性时通过保守回退和公平校正控制损失与曝光差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习增强算法能够利用预测改进在线决策，但不可靠建议可能降低效率并加剧曝光不公平。有限候选集、不可逆决策和曝光约束进一步增加了设计兼顾性能与公平性的在线算法的难度。

Method: 提出一种结合预测建议、保守回退机制与公平校正的鲁棒公平分配规则。在有界预测误差假设下，建立算法性能的理论保证，重点分析预测误差对损失的影响，并通过实验评估其在对抗性建议和曝光公平性方面的表现。

Result: 理论上，算法同时满足一致性与鲁棒性：预测准确时可获得相应收益，预测存在误差时性能损失与预测误差成正比。实验表明，该方法在对抗性建议下保持较高稳定性，并显著降低不同候选对象之间的曝光差异。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将预测信息与保守回退及公平校正结合，可以在在线分配中实现效率、鲁棒性和公平性的平衡。方法的有效性依赖于有界误差假设，后续仍需研究更弱误差条件及更复杂动态场景下的适用性。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms improve online decisions using predictions, but unreliable advice may harm efficiency and fairness. We study an online allocation problem with finite candidate sets, irreversible decisions, and exposure constraints. We propose a robust and fair rule combining advice with a conservative fallback and fairness correction. Under bounded-error assumptions, we prove consistency and robustness with loss proportional to prediction error. Experiments show stability under adversarial advice and significant reductions in exposure disparity.

</details>


### [73] [AI agents in Algorithmic Electricity Markets: On the Emergence of Tacit Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26896)
*Jakub Seredyński,Georgios Tsaousoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究采用自主学习算法进行竞价的电力市场中，是否会在未被明确指示合作的情况下出现默许协同行为。结果表明，在部分实验条件下，多智能体确实能够学习并维持高于竞争水平的市场结果，存在符合默许协同判据的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着市场参与者使用学习型智能体制定报价策略，电力市场的算法化程度提高。该类市场通常具有参与者数量较少、反复互动等结构特征，因此需要评估独立学习过程是否可能导致非竞争性的价格与收益结果。

Method: 作者将策略报价建模为具有不完全公开监测的重复博弈，并使用多智能体强化学习刻画参与者的涌现行为。为识别默许协同，论文提出多维评估标准，不仅比较智能体利润与纳什均衡下的利润，还综合考察市场结果是否持续呈现超竞争性特征及其他协同迹象。

Result: 实验显示，某些设定下，学习智能体能够形成并持续维持超竞争性结果；这些结果满足所提出的多项默许协同指标。该现象发生时，智能体并未被预先编程或训练为进行协同。

Conclusion: 自主学习报价算法可能在特定电力市场环境中自发产生接近默许协同的行为。论文指出，评估此类风险不能仅依赖利润相对纳什均衡的比较，而应采用包含行为持续性、市场结果与监测条件在内的多维判据。

Abstract: As electricity market participants increasingly adopt learning-based agents for their bidding strategies, electricity markets are becoming algorithmic. Evidence from algorithmic markets in other domains shows that tacit collusion can arise purely through independent learning. Moreover, electricity markets are typically oligopolistic and feature repeated interaction among a small number of participants, making them structurally susceptible to non-competitive behavior. In the face of these observations, this paper investigates the hypothesis that tacit collusion may emerge in electricity markets where participants' actions are controlled by autonomous learning-based algorithms. We model strategic bidding as a repeated game with imperfect public monitoring, and model the participants' emergent behavior using multi-agent reinforcement learning. We propose a multi-dimensional set of criteria (going beyond profit comparisons against Nash equilibria) to assess whether the resulting behavior constitutes tacit collusion. Our experimental results showcase that such a danger is realistic for electricity markets: there are cases where agents do learn to sustain supra-competitive outcomes that are supportive of tacit collusion indicators, even though the agents were never instructed to collude.

</details>


### [74] [Counterfactual Bias Testing for Application Tracking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26899)
*Sai Yashwant,Shruti Bansal,Anurag Dubey,Samaroha Chatterjee,Satyam Kumar,Shreyash Gupta,Gantala Thulsiram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于任务专用大语言模型代理的自动化候选人—职位匹配公平性审计方法，通过生成身份中立简历、注入受控人口特征处理，并结合多指标统计检验识别潜在偏差。实验表明，仅依赖分数或留存率可能遗漏由排名稳定性和nDCG指标暴露的边界问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对应审计依赖人工编写简历和手动投递，成本高、难以适应候选人匹配系统的快速迭代。研究旨在构建可复用、低成本、可扩展的自动化审计流程，以支持高风险人工智能系统的持续公平性评估。

Method: 方法首先利用任务专用LLM代理生成身份中立的基础简历，并沿性别、年龄、居住地、语言和残障五个受保护特征轴注入受控处理，形成K×(1+N)对应审计矩阵。随后依据与欧盟人工智能法案对齐的提示词标记推断出的受保护特征，使用微调句向量模型和余弦相似度完成职位匹配。公平性评估覆盖反事实指标、群体公平指标和基于绩效的公平指标，共九类指标，并配套自助法置信区间、显著性检验及Benjamini-Hochberg多重比较校正，最终生成PASS、INVESTIGATE或FAIL报告及综合风险分数。

Result: 在包含5个职位订单、100名基础候选人和10种人口特征处理的示例语料上，共完成90组指标—变体评估。分数变化、Top-K留存率和基于绩效的公平性差距均处于容差范围内，但平均绝对排名变化（MARC）和nDCG@K分别发现边界性问题，其中一项甚至出现在中性基线中。这说明仅观察分数或留存率会漏检部分排名与排序质量异常。

Conclusion: 多指标、跨指标族的审计比单一综合分数更适合评估候选人—职位匹配系统的公平性。LLM代理生成的审计材料不能完全替代人工策划和验证，但可作为一种实用、低成本且适合持续迭代流程的补充方案。

Abstract: Automated candidate-job matching systems are increasingly classified as high-risk AI under emerging regulation, yet auditing them for demographic bias is expensive: classical correspondence-audit studies require hand-crafted resumes and manual submission, which does not scale to fast pipeline retraining cycles. This paper presents a general, reusable methodology that (1) uses task-specialized LLM agents to synthesize identity-neutral base resumes and inject controlled demographic treatments across five protected-characteristic axes (sex/gender, age, residence, language, disability), producing a K x (1+N) correspondence-audit matrix; (2) qualitatively flags inferred protected characteristics per an EU AI Act-aligned prompt; (3) ranks candidates against a job description via a fine-tuned sentence-embedding model and cosine similarity; and (4) computes a nine-metric fairness suite spanning counterfactual (score delta, mean absolute rank change, flip rate), group-fairness (top-K retention, four-fifths/impact ratio), and merit-aware (Recall@K, nDCG@K, equal opportunity, equalized odds) families, each with bootstrap confidence intervals, significance tests, and Benjamini-Hochberg correction, culminating in an automated PASS/INVESTIGATE/FAIL report with a composite risk score. On an example corpus of 5 job orders, 100 base candidates, and 10 demographic treatments (90 metric x variant evaluations): score shifts, top-K retention, and merit-aware rate gaps stay within tolerance for every treatment, but a rank-stability metric (MARC) and nDCG@K each surface borderline findings - including one on the neutral baseline itself - that a score- or retention-only view would miss. The results argue for multi-metric, multi-family auditing over any single aggregate score, and for LLM-agent-generated audits as a practical, low-cost complement to human-curated audits for any candidate-job matching pipeline.

</details>


### [75] [A Table Is Worth 64 Tokens: Pixel-level Compression for Multi-Table Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26949)
*Iñigo Alonso,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究将多表格文档压缩为图像后进行问答的效果，发现原生分辨率表格可在性能与效率上匹配文本表示，而过度缩放会损害可读性并诱发低效推理。基于表格相关性识别与原生分辨率精读的两步方法，在长文档上节省41%的总token，并提升7个准确率百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实文档问答通常需要同时处理长文本和多个表格。将上下文表示为图像有望降低视觉token成本，但像素级表格压缩对表格理解、推理效率和问答准确率的影响尚不明确，因此有必要系统评估不同压缩程度及其适用场景。

Method: 在包含多个表格的文档问答任务中，研究者评估5种视觉语言模型、2个基准数据集和5种视觉token预算。实验比较原生分辨率表格、不同程度下采样表格与文本表示的性能和token效率。进一步提出无需训练的两步方法：第一步在像素压缩的完整上下文中识别与问题相关的表格；第二步仅对相关表格使用原生分辨率进行问答推理。

Result: 原生分辨率表格在准确率和效率上均可达到与文本表示相当的水平。下采样会降低表格可读性，使模型产生更长且效果较差的推理轨迹，从而抵消预期的token节省。但高度下采样的表格仍保留足够信息用于判断相关性。两步方法在长文档上相比使用原生分辨率表格的一步式问答节省41%的总token，并提升7个准确率百分点；相比token效率最高的一步式压缩配置，进一步减少15%的token，同时保持准确率不变。

Conclusion: 表格图像压缩存在明显的不对称性：低分辨率足以支持相关性筛选，却不适合精确推理。将低成本的表格检索与原生分辨率的局部推理结合，可以同时降低计算开销并提高问答性能，说明分阶段视觉上下文分配是长文档多表格问答的有效策略。

Abstract: Answering questions over real-world documents requires processing long inputs that interleave text with tables. Optical context compression, which represents context as images, promises to reduce token cost, but its effect on table understanding remains unclear. We study pixel-level table compression for question answering over documents with multiple tables, evaluating five VLMs across two benchmarks and five visual-token budgets. Representing tables as images at native resolution matches text in both performance and efficiency, but downscaling them makes models compensate the loss in readability with longer, less effective reasoning traces that cancel the expected savings. Highly downscaled tables, however, preserve enough signal to identify whether they are relevant to a question. We exploit this asymmetry with a training-free, two-step method: the model first identifies the tables needed to answer a question from a pixel-compressed context, and then reasons over those at native resolution. On long documents, our method saves 41% of total tokens and gains 7 accuracy points over single-step QA with native resolution tables. It also uses 15% fewer tokens than the most efficient single-step compressed configuration, with no accuracy loss.

</details>


### [76] [From Atomic to Agentic: Towards Interpretable Evaluation of LLMs' Agentic Mathematical Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26950)
*Jiayi Kuang,Yinghui Li,Yunze Song,Keyu Chen,Zhifeng Shen,Yangning Li,Yidong Wang,Di Yin,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Kai Jin,Ying Shen,Liang Lin,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向大语言模型数学推理过程的评测基准，不再仅依据最终答案判断能力，而是系统评估模型在规划、执行与反馈等环节中的智能体式数学能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准大多采用结果导向评测，只检查答案是否正确，难以定位模型在问题分解、步骤选择、工具性操作、错误识别和修正等推理过程中的具体缺陷，也无法充分衡量其作为可靠数学智能体的潜力。

Method: 作者将数学解题中的智能体行为映射到一套可复用的数学原子能力分类体系，并构建覆盖规划、行动和反馈三类任务的评测套件。任务同时包含文本与多模态场景；数据构建采用自动化流水线生成高质量解题轨迹，并通过受控的大语言模型改写获得细粒度过程标注。

Result: 实验显示，即使多个模型在端到端答题准确率上接近，其在不同智能体能力维度上的表现仍可能存在显著差异。这说明单一最终答案指标会掩盖模型推理机制和过程可靠性方面的重要差别。

Conclusion: 过程级评测对于理解大语言模型真实的数学智能体能力至关重要。该基准能够提供更细致的能力画像与失效诊断，为训练、选择和优化下一代数学推理智能体提供更具操作性的依据。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are evolving from performing end-to-end mathematical reasoning to integrating agentic intelligence. However, most existing math benchmarks evaluate only final answers. This outcome-oriented evaluation provides limited diagnostic value for identifying process-level failures or rigorous logic, failing to guide the transformation of LLMs into robust agents. To bridge this gap, we present a process-level benchmark designed to evaluate the inherent agentic mathematical reasoning abilities of LLMs. Our framework aligns problem-solving agentic behaviors with a structured taxonomy of reusable mathematical atomic capabilities. We design a comprehensive suite of planning, action, and feedback tasks across both textual and multimodal contexts, supported by an automated pipeline that synthesizes high-quality trajectories and produces fine-grained annotations via controlled LLM rewriting. Experiments reveal that models with similar end-to-end accuracy can exhibit markedly different agentic capability profiles. This demonstrates that process-level evaluation is crucial for interpreting the true potential of LLMs and guiding the development of next-generation mathematical agents.

</details>


### [77] [GraphMemix: Query-Aware Evidence Forests for Long-Term Multimodal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26983)
*Geng Li,Yuhao Wang,Dong Li,Jianye Hao,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphMemix将多模态长期记忆组织建模为查询感知的证据森林构建问题，通过候选图扩展、证据效用与激活成本建模以及森林优化，在有限证据预算下选择结构可靠、信息互补且低冗余的记忆上下文，从而同时提升准确率并降低记忆生命周期成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆方法主要存在两类问题：问题无关的离线摘要计算和维护成本高，基于嵌入相似度的检索又容易产生上下文不完整、信息冗余或冲突。尤其是低相似度但具有补充价值的证据难以被发现，因此需要一种面向查询、能够利用关系结构并控制成本的记忆组织机制。

Method: GraphMemix包含三个核心步骤。第一，候选图构建：从多视角种子记忆出发，通过模式关系和语义关系扩展候选图，以获得与当前查询相关的原始上下文。第二，证据效用与激活成本建模：将记忆对查询的直接支持与基于锚点的关系验证解耦，用于抑制冗余或相互冲突的信息。第三，森林优化：在最大证据预算约束下，联合选择森林格式的记忆上下文及其可靠关系结构，将记忆检索转化为组合优化问题。

Result: 在四个长期多模态记忆基准上的实验表明，GraphMemix在不同基础模型配置下均取得显著性能提升，并在任务准确率与记忆生命周期成本之间建立了新的Pareto前沿。摘要还表明，该方法能够恢复传统相似度检索容易遗漏的低相似度互补证据。

Conclusion: GraphMemix通过查询感知的子图组织替代静态摘要或简单相似度匹配，使长期多模态记忆能够以更低的生命周期成本提供更完整、更可靠且更少冗余的上下文。其主要贡献在于将记忆选择、关系验证和成本控制统一到证据森林优化框架中；不过摘要未说明具体优化算法、成本定义、绝对指标提升幅度及计算开销，仍需结合正文评估方法的可扩展性与实际部署代价。

Abstract: Organizing long-term memory for multimodal agents remains challenging because existing methods either suffer from expensive question-agnostic offline summaries or naive embedding similarity matching that introduces incomplete and redundant context. To address these issues, we propose GraphMemix, a combinatorial-optimization graph memory framework that models memory organization as query-aware evidence-forest construction. Specifically, our method consists of three key components:(1) candidate graph construction, which expands multi-view seed memories through schema and semantic relations to acquire query-aware original context; (2) evidence utility and activation costs, which decouples direct memory support from anchor-conditioned relation verification to suppress redundant or conflicting information; and (3) forest optimization, which jointly selects a forest-format memory context under a maximum evidence budget and its reliable relational structure. By organizing memory into a query-relevant subgraph, the method avoids substantial lifecycle cost and recovers low-similarity complementary evidence. Experimental results across four long-term multimodal memory benchmarks demonstrate significant improvements with different foundation models and establish a new Pareto frontier between accuracy and lifecycle cost.

</details>


### [78] [DSA: Evidence-Aware LLM-Agent Orchestration for Multi-Market Stock Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26990)
*Linsen Zhu,Yi Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DSA：一个面向多市场股票研究的、证据感知型LLM智能体编排框架。其重点并非证明投资预测能力，而是通过证据获取、上下文构建、模型路由、角色/策略推理、报告校验与风险控制，提高研究流程的可追溯性和软件契约一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯由大语言模型生成金融摘要难以满足可运行股票研究系统的要求：系统需整合异构证据，明确数据与模型能力边界，并限制生成观点对最终报告的影响。因此，作者试图构建一个具有证据约束、诊断信息、输出验证和风险保护机制的端到端编排框架。

Method: DSA将流程划分为证据采集、结构化上下文构建、按能力路由的模型分析、可选的角色推理与Strategy Skill推理、以及结合选定上下文和诊断信息的报告生成。系统提供默认报告模式和智能体模式，两者复用证据及模型路由服务，但采用不同的输出验证和风险保障。智能体模式中，核心角色输出经由角色专用解析器处理；Strategy Skill观点还需经过信号资格划分，再进行综合。系统将意见分歧显式传递给决策智能体，并在后续施加保守的风险覆盖规则。参考实现覆盖六条区域市场路径、十五项内置Strategy Skill、托管与本地模型路由，以及多种执行和交付接口。

Result: 在冻结的软件快照上，作者选定的1,457项可移植离线后端契约测试全部通过；其中596项测试被回溯映射至与所述LLM智能体架构密切相关的六类契约。该结果表明实现对被测软件接口和行为契约具有一致性支持，并展示了框架在多市场、多模型和多角色工作流中的工程覆盖范围。

Conclusion: DSA的主要贡献是提供可审计、可路由且带有风险控制的LLM股票研究编排基础设施。测试证据仅能支持其软件契约实现符合预期，不能据此推断报告质量更高、预测更准确或能够获得更优的投资回报。

Abstract: Large language models can summarize financial information, but an operational stock-research system must first assemble heterogeneous evidence, expose unavailable data and model capabilities, and control how generated opinions affect a final report. We present DSA, an evidence-aware orchestration framework for multi-market stock research with large language model (LLM) agents. DSA organizes the workflow into evidence acquisition, structured context construction, model-routed analysis, optional role and Strategy Skill reasoning, and report generation with selected context and diagnostics. A default report profile and an optional agentic profile share evidence and model-routing services but use profile-specific output validation and risk safeguards. In the agentic profile, core role outputs are processed by role-specific parsers, whereas Strategy Skill opinions undergo an additional signal-eligibility partition before synthesis; disagreement is supplied explicitly to the decision agent, followed by a conservative risk override. The reference implementation includes six regional market paths, fifteen bundled Strategy Skills, hosted and local model routes, and multiple execution and delivery surfaces. At a frozen software snapshot, a selected manifest of 1,457 portable offline backend contract tests passed; 596 cases were retrospectively mapped to six contract families central to the reported LLM-agent architecture. This evidence establishes implementation conformance for the tested software contracts, not superior report quality, forecasting accuracy, or investment returns.

</details>


### [79] [ASIL: Replacing Screenshot-and-Click with Structured State and Semantic Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26991)
*Rui Xie,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AS​​IL，一种面向智能体的软件交互层，通过结构化JSON观测和可执行的语义动作替代截图点击，使软件操作更适合长期规划；在15个应用和380项任务上显著提升执行成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截图信息不完整，点击操作脆弱且语义表达能力弱，难以支持复杂、长期的软件操作任务。现有代码智能体虽能执行脚本和调用工具，但对主要依赖图形界面的软件访问能力不足，因此需要一种更结构化、语义化的交互接口。

Method: 设计AS​​IL，根据每个应用可获得的最深访问路径，将软件能力封装为结构化JSON状态观测和代码可执行的语义动作。在15个应用上构建包含300项单应用任务和80项多应用任务的基准，并与截图点击、LibreOffice UNO API及draw.io MCP内容接口进行比较；同时通过监督微调和资源受限的在线强化学习评估其训练价值。

Result: 闭源模型使用AS​​IL时任务成功率超过80%，平均每项任务少于5次动作。相同任务在修复运行环境和50步截图预算下，截图点击的严格成功率仅为单应用6.6、多应用26.6；在较宽松的OSWorld可比任务集上分别为15.0和53.3。匹配任务中，AS​​IL较LibreOffice UNO API高出28至38个百分点，但与draw.io MCP内容接口基本相当。训练方面，监督微调使Qwen3.5-2B和9B分别从58.0、66.6提升至72.1、80.4；在线强化学习进一步提升至74.4、82.2。

Conclusion: AS​​IL通过结构化状态和语义动作显著降低了软件操作的动作成本，并提高了复杂任务的可靠性与可规划性。结果表明，面向智能体设计的软件接口优于通用截图点击方式；但其优势依赖应用适配深度，针对已有高质量原生内容接口的改进空间相对有限。

Abstract: Powerful code agents can execute scripts, call tools, and manage files, yet many important applications remain accessible primarily through graphical user interfaces. We argue that screenshot-and-click is an inefficient interface for software-operating agents: screenshots are state-incomplete, and GUI actions are brittle, semantically weak, and poorly matched to long-horizon planning. We introduce ASIL (Agent-Software Interaction Layer), an agent-native interface that exposes software through structured JSON observations and code-executable semantic actions, realized through the deepest feasible access path for each application. We instantiate ASIL across 15 applications and a benchmark of 300 single-application and 80 multi-application tasks. ASIL reaches above 80 with closed models while executing fewer than five actions per task. Under a repaired runtime and a 50-step screenshot budget, the same tasks yield 6.6 and 26.6 strict success under screenshot-and-click control, rising to 15.0 and 53.3 on an easier OSWorld-comparable band. Against application-native interfaces on matched tasks, ASIL exceeds LibreOffice's UNO API by 28-38 strict points but only matches draw.io's MCP content contract. The structured modality also suits training: small-scale SFT raises Qwen3.5-2B from 58.0 to 72.1 and Qwen3.5-9B from 66.6 to 80.4, and resource-limited on-policy RL further raises them to 74.4 and 82.2.

</details>

*Varvara Mama,Eleni Veroni,Nikolaos Kapsalis,Christos D. Nikolopoulos,Anargyros T. Baklezos*

Abstract: In the present work an efficient border control management procedure is proposed. Compared to operational queue management systems, whose operations are based on mostly static data, the proposed work takes into account dynamic traffic conditions, thus enabling optimal performance, even in cases of uncertainty. To this end, we are proposing a multi-modal Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework, tailored to th needs of border control systems, which enables real-time queue prediction, management, and resource optimization. The novel proposed approach integrates heterogeneous data sources and presents them through a unified representation by employing Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks for queue forecasting. Furthermore, it leverages Model Predictive Control (MPC) and scheduling optimization to derive actionable control policies, which in turn can be presented to border control officers. The proposed work has been evaluated using synthetic data simulating realistic traffic. The evaluation results demonstrate that the proposed method reduces queue prediction error by up to 35% and average waiting time by 30%. Accordingly, the average throughput increases by nearly 20%, compared to ARIMA and rule-based methods. The abovementioned results show the effectiveness and efficiency of combining AI architectures with optimization techniques for proactive and adaptive border traffic management.

</details>


### [81] [Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27044)
*Wei-Yao Wang,Kazuya Tateishi,Shuyang Cui,Christian Simon,Takashi Shibuya,Shusuke Takahashi,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniUE，一种支持文本、视频和音频统一表示学习及多模态交互查询的通用嵌入模型，并构建OmniCHOIR评测其组合式音频检索能力。实验显示，OmniUE在文本、音频、视觉交互及全模态基准上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的嵌入器主要覆盖文本和图像，交互形式也以语言或图像为主，难以统一处理视频、音频以及更细粒度的视觉区域和音频片段查询。因此，研究需要构建同时支持多模态表示和用户条件交互的通用嵌入框架。

Method: OmniUE利用专门可学习令牌的中间层表示，在文本、视频和音频之间学习统一嵌入空间。视觉分割器和音频分割器分别处理用户指定的视觉感兴趣区域与音频片段，并将这些交互输入与全模态大语言模型结合，通过上下文聚合生成用户条件化的任意到任意嵌入。作者同时提出OmniCHOIR，用于评测基于文本、视频、音频及单模态或多模态交互提示的组合式音频检索。

Result: 相较于当前最优基线，OmniUE在MMEB-v2-video文本交互视频基准上平均提升10.5%，在MAEB音频任务上提升1.1%，在SCaR视觉交互基准上提升83.7%，在OmniCHOIR全模态交互基准上提升24.1%。结果表明，该模型在多种模态和交互方式下具有稳定优势。

Conclusion: OmniUE验证了将全模态表示学习与全模态交互查询联合建模的有效性，为构建支持复杂用户交互的通用嵌入器提供了可行路径。其主要贡献包括统一文本、视频、音频嵌入空间、支持区域和片段级交互，以及提出相应的OmniCHOIR评测基准。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning has been shifting from traditional two-tower architectures to large language model (LLM)-based embedders due to their strong instruction-following capabilities. Despite this progress, existing approaches primarily focus on language and image modalities, which also remain the dominant modalities for user-conditioned interactions in current embedders. In this paper, we propose the first Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder (OmniUE), which not only learns a unified embedding space across text, video, and audio by leveraging intermediate-layer representations from dedicated learnable tokens, but also supports omni-interactive querying, enabling users to provide inputs in the form of text, visual regions of interest, and audio spans. Within OmniUE, visual and audio segmenters process diverse user interactions and integrate them with an omni-LLM to produce user-conditioned any-to-any embeddings via context aggregation. To evaluate OmniUE's omni-interactive capabilities, we introduce OmniCHOIR, benchmarking models for omni-interactive compositional audio retrieval based on the given text, video, and audio as well as unimodal or multimodal interaction prompts. OmniUE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art baselines across diverse modalities, with average improvements of 10.5% on textual-interactive video benchmarks (MMEB-v2-video), 1.1% on audio tasks (MAEB), 83.7% on visual-interactive benchmarks (SCaR), and 24.1% on our omni-interactive OmniCHOIR benchmark. We believe that jointly advancing omni-modal representation learning and omni-interactive querying paves the way toward universal embedders.

</details>


### [82] [pro-team at LLMs4OL 2026 Tasks Flagship and Reuse: Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Vocabulary-Constrained Filtering for Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27101)
*Shivam Mishra,Dhannu Ram Meena,Muneendra Ojha,Krishna Pratap Singh,Kuldeep Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线检索增强少样本提示方法，用于LLM驱动的文本本体学习，覆盖端到端本体构建与本体扩展复用两个任务。方法在任务B上取得较高性能，但未能抽取非分类关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本本体学习仍面临领域术语幻觉、输出格式不一致以及模型偏好层次关系而忽视关联关系等问题。研究旨在通过检索示例、上下文管理和规则化后处理，提高LLM生成本体术语、类型和关系的准确性与一致性。

Method: 系统采用Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct作为生成模型，使用all-MiniLM-L6-v2检索相似示例。任务A选取前5个示例，任务B选取前2个示例；通过左截断上下文窗口保留任务指令。任务B进一步对生成三元组执行确定性词汇约束过滤：当三元组至少一个端点属于样本的封闭术语或类型词汇表时予以保留，并移除初始本体中已有的重复三元组。

Result: 任务B取得0.8692的语义图相似度、0.9200的术语类型判定F1和0.8540的分类体系发现F1；任务A的语义图相似度为0.7416。实验表明，词汇约束和去重后处理有助于提升本体扩展质量，但系统未抽取任何非分类关系。

Conclusion: 离线检索增强少样本提示结合封闭词汇过滤，能够有效改善LLM在本体术语、类型和分类关系上的表现，尤其适用于本体扩展任务。然而，该方法的关系词汇设计具有明显的分类导向，限制了关联关系发现能力。后续研究应扩大关系类型覆盖范围，并引入能够识别开放域非分类语义关系的机制。

Abstract: Ontology learning from text remains challenging despite significant progress in Large Language Models (LLMs), which can hallucinate domain terms, produce inconsistent formats, and favor hierarchical over associative relations. In the LLMs4OL 2026 Challenge, we address both the End-to-End Flagship Task (Task A) and Ontology Extension Reuse Task (Task B) using an offline retrieval-augmented few-shot prompting pipeline. Our system employs Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct with all-MiniLM-L6-v2 for demonstration retrieval, selecting the top-5 examples for Task A and top-2 for Task B. A left-truncated context-windowing strategy preserves task instructions within long prompts. For Task B, generated triples undergo deterministic vocabulary-constrained filtering, retaining triples when at least one endpoint belongs to the sample's closed term/type vocabulary and removing duplicates of the initial ontology. The approach achieves Semantic Graph Similarity of 0.8692, Term-Typing F1 of 0.9200, and Taxonomy Discovery F1 of 0.8540 on Task B, while Task A achieves 0.7416 Semantic Graph Similarity. However, no non-taxonomic relations are extracted, highlighting limitations of closed, taxonomy-oriented relation vocabularies.

</details>


### [83] [TransMeme: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Cultural Meme Transcreation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27127)
*Jingyi Zheng,Yule Liu,Zifan Peng,Tianyi Hu,Yuemeng Zhao,Xinhu Zheng,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究中英双向表情包跨文化再创作，提出由多个专职智能体协同完成文化适配、文本改写、修订及必要视觉调整的框架。在人工评测和大模型评测中均优于基线方法，主要剩余难点是幽默重构与图文对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表情包是普遍的多模态在线交流形式，但其含义高度依赖语言、文化语境及图文关系。传统翻译或单独文本改写难以同时保留原始交际意图、适配目标受众的文化理解，并维持文本与图像的一致性，因此需要专门的跨文化再创作方法。

Method: 论文首先将任务拆解为三项核心挑战：文化特定知识理解、意图与语气保持、图文多模态一致性。随后构建多智能体框架，由不同专职智能体分别执行文化适配、目标文本重写、结果修订和条件性视觉调整；智能体之间通过协调反馈强化文本适配，并处理需要更深层文化解释或视觉干预的复杂样例。实验采用中英双向表情包再创作，并结合人工评测与大模型裁判评测。

Result: 所提方法在两类评测设置下均稳定超过全部基线。人工评测中，方法在四个维度上均取得最佳表现，相较最强基线平均提升33.1%。大模型裁判评测中，其Top-1排名率达到60%，而第二名基线为26%。组件分析表明，框架中的各个模块均对最终性能具有贡献。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作能够有效提升跨文化表情包再创作的文化适配性、意图保真度和多模态连贯性。当前性能瓶颈并不主要来自基础文化知识缺失，而集中于幽默效果的跨语言重构及图文语义对齐；后续研究应重点探索更可靠的幽默迁移与视觉—文本联合编辑能力。

Abstract: Internet memes are a pervasive form of multimodal online communication; however, such communication often involves users from diverse linguistic and cultural backgrounds. Therefore, adapting memes across cultures and languages is a central challenge for enabling mutual understanding in online communication. Unlike ordinary translation or standalone text rewriting, cross-cultural meme transcreation must jointly preserve communicative intent, adapt culture-dependent meaning for the target audience, and maintain coherence between text and image. In this work, we first provide an explicit task analysis of cross-cultural meme transcreation and identify three core challenges: culture-specific knowledge understanding, intent and tone preservation, and multimodal consistency. Based on this analysis, we propose a multi-agent framework with specialized agents that are coordinated to address these challenges through cultural adaptation, target text rewriting, revision, and conditional visual adjustment. The framework strengthens target text adaptation with coordinated feedback to handle difficult cases that require deeper cultural or visual intervention. We evaluate the framework on bidirectional Chinese-English meme transcreation using both human evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge. Our method consistently outperforms all baselines across both evaluation settings. In human evaluation, it achieves the best performance on all four dimensions and delivers a 33.1% average improvement over the strongest baseline, while in LLM-as-a-Judge, it attains the highest Top-1 ranking rate (60% versus 26% for the second-best baseline). Further analysis indicates that each component contributes to the performance. Our error analysis suggests that the remaining bottlenecks lie in humor reconstruction and image-text alignment rather than simple cultural knowledge gaps, pointing to future work on humor transfer.

</details>


### [84] [GRAIN: Bridging Name and Narrative Shifts in Real-World Graph Reasoning through Invariance-Rewarded Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27142)
*Zike Yuan,Han Zhang,Jianzhi Yan,Le Liu,Cai Ke,Huozhi Zhou,Jian Xie,Jiran Yin,Yukun Cao,Yue Yu,Hui Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAIN，一个通过强化学习优化的单智能体框架，使大语言模型能够从噪声文本中稳健地解析图结构并执行图工具。其在准确率、推理延迟和分布外结构泛化方面均优于多智能体及监督微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在节点标识符变化和任务表述变化下容易依赖表面语言模式，导致图结构解析脆弱。传统确定性图工具虽然具有结构不变性，但输入拓扑通常需要先从噪声文本中提取，而多智能体方案又带来较高延迟。因此，研究目标是以较低推理成本提升文本到图结构映射的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Method: GRAIN将推理建模为“语义解析—工具执行”流水线，并通过强化学习进行优化。核心是Structure Invariance Reward：将模型提取的中间图与真实拓扑进行验证和比较，奖励结构保持一致的解析结果，从而抑制对语言表面特征的记忆。研究同时提出GRIT基准，用于评估模型对节点标识符和语言表述变化的敏感性。

Result: GRAIN相较多智能体基线准确率提升16.45%，推理延迟降低约24%。在分布外泛化方面，监督微调模型的OOD性能差距由15.77%降至7.80%，约减少一半；在超出训练规模的大型图上，GRAIN仍保持较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，面向中间图结构的强化学习奖励能够有效诱导大语言模型学习稳健的文本到拓扑映射，而非拟合语言伪特征。单智能体GRAIN在效率、准确率和结构泛化之间取得了较好的平衡，但摘要未说明奖励设计的具体实现、训练成本、基准规模及统计显著性，这些因素仍需通过完整实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Despite their potential in standardized graph tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain brittle to real-world shifts in node identifiers and task formulation. While deterministic graph tools are invariant to such shifts, extracting topological structures from noisy text is highly fragile for LLMs, which often overfit to surface patterns. Moreover, mitigating these parsing failures via multi-agent systems incurs prohibitive latency. To address this, we propose GRAIN, a single-agent framework optimized via reinforcement learning. GRAIN models reasoning as a semantic parsing and tool-execution pipeline, guided by a Structure Invariance Reward. By validating extracted intermediate graphs against ground-truth topologies, this reward forces the LLM to learn robust text-to-structure mappings rather than memorizing linguistic artifacts. We also introduce GRIT, a benchmark evaluating sensitivity to such linguistic shifts. GRAIN outperforms multi-agent baselines by 16.45\% in accuracy with approximately 24\% lower latency. Furthermore, it demonstrates superior structural generalization, halving the out-of-distribution (OOD) gap of SFT models (from 15.77\% to 7.80\%) and maintaining robustness on large-scale graphs beyond the training distribution.

</details>


### [85] [Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27144)
*Xiang Wang,Zhijun Cheng,Zhenyu Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JPGFN，一种结合特征变换、自适应Jacobi多项式图滤波和节点标签约束的图异常检测方法，旨在提升频域建模能力、细粒度特征学习能力及标签利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于频域滤波的图异常检测方法存在三方面不足：静态基函数难以适应图数据的频域分布；未充分建模节点特征中不同属性的重要性，导致细粒度信息丢失；对节点标签的利用不足，限制了检测性能。

Method: 方法由三个模块组成：1）特征分离变换网络（FSTNN）对不同维度的节点特征进行分离，并施加非线性变换，以学习更具区分性的细粒度表示；2）基于Jacobi多项式构建自适应图滤波模块，以适应图信号的频域分布并捕获复杂频域特征；3）节点标签约束模块将标签信息引入训练过程，增强异常检测能力。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，JPGFN整体性能显著优于主流图异常检测方法，说明其在特征表示、频域滤波和标签利用方面具有综合优势。摘要未报告具体数据集、评价指标或性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: JPGFN通过联合优化细粒度特征变换、自适应频域滤波与节点标签约束，有效缓解了现有方法的三项关键局限，为图异常检测提供了更灵活的频域建模框架。但其泛化性、模块独立贡献及对标签质量的敏感性仍需通过更详细的消融实验和稳健性分析验证。

Abstract: In recent years, graph anomaly detection (GAD) based on frequency-domain filtering have achieved promising results. However, existing approaches still face three major challenges: First, they use static basic function to constructed graph filter which cannot effectively adapt to the frequency-domain distribution of graph data. Second, they fail to adequately consider the importance information of each attribute in the node feature vector, leading to the loss of fine-grained information. Third, they insufficiently utilize node labels for GAD. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel graph anomaly detection method called JPGFN (Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering Network). First, a Feature Separation Transformation Network (FSTNN) is developed to better learn fine-grained node features by feature separation and applying nonlinear transformations to node features across different dimensions. Second, an adaptive Jacobi polynomial graph filtering module is constructed based on Jacobi polynomials to adaptively capture complex frequency-domain features of graph signals. Finally, a node label constraint module is developed to facilitate the use of node labels and enhance the performance of GAD. Experimental results on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms mainstream approaches.

</details>


### [86] [Thomson: Continual Learning of Frontier Models for SovereignAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27147)
*Shengzhuang Chen,Jerrod Parker,Yejin Bang,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat,Stefan Winzeck,Daniil Glazko,Jannik Zgraggen,Fangyi Yu,Scott Arnott,Dietrich Trautmann,Luca Ciuffreda,Guglielmo Bonifazi,Davide Romano,Bradley Bell,Kirsty Fielding,Daniele Giofrè,Tom Zielund,Ipshita Chatterjee,Sneha Murthy Ghantasala,Manpreet Nanreh,John Scoville,Maciej Sakowicz,Wassim Seifeddine,Lukas Thede,Jonathan Richard Schwarz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张：机构无需从头训练超大模型，可基于开放权重模型实施持续学习，并结合现代中后训练流程与参数保护机制，以较低算力和人员成本获得接近前沿模型的能力，同时减少领域适配中的灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为，前沿模型开发通常集中于资源充足的少数主体，导致模型能力、数据控制与部署治理方面的不对称。现有关于“自主人工智能能力”的讨论缺少适用于不同预算条件的短期实施路径；而提示工程、小规模微调和冻结模型的工具增强，往往难以实现广泛且稳定的能力提升。

Method: 方法以可获得的开放权重模型为起点，采用持续学习而非单一领域微调。训练流程覆盖现代中训练与后训练阶段，并在各阶段引入兼顾可塑性与稳定性的保护机制；核心原则是在参数上进行尽可能少但影响显著的干预，以在学习新能力时保留原有通用能力。作者据此训练了通用模型Thomson，并重点强化高要求专业任务能力。

Result: Thomson在智能体任务、安全性、法律、税务、多语言及大规模深度研究等评测中，与近期前沿模型表现接近。结果呈现“π形”改善：多类能力同步提升，且部分未被直接优化的能力也获得增益；与此同时，窄领域适配中常见的灾难性遗忘被大幅缓解。作者声称，其效果可与多个连续模型代际通常带来的提升相当，但所需计算和人力预算显著更低。

Conclusion: 论文的主要结论是，利用开放权重模型、持续学习和稳定性保护的中后训练方案，更多资源有限的机构也可能自主拥有模型、工具基础设施、价值对齐与数据隐私等关键能力。该结论具有实践吸引力，但摘要未提供具体模型规模、数据构成、训练成本、对照基线及评测细节，因此其成本优势、泛化能力和“前沿级”定位仍需依赖完整论文中的可复现实验进一步验证。

Abstract: The development of frontier models is commonly perceived to be the exclusive remit of a small number of heavily funded players, creating an information, economic and power asymmetry between developers and the diverse user base of modern AI. Recent public discourse acknowledges this concern, calling for SovereignAI (an organisation's capability to independently build, deploy and govern AI use), but offers little concrete advice on how this can be achieved in the short term under a diversity of funding settings. We argue that frontier performance is achievable by a wide range of institutions through Continual Learning on readily available open-weight models. Unlike limited approaches such as small-scale fine-tuning, prompt engineering, or tool-augmentation of a frozen model, our approach exploits a modern mid- & post-training stack while introducing safeguards that preserve both plasticity and stability at each stage, making the minimal number of high-impact interventions on the parameters. This yields gains comparable to those typically seen across multiple successive model generations, at compute and personnel budgets substantially lower than commonly thought, making ownership of large parts of the SovereignAI stack (model, tool infrastructure, values & data privacy) viable for far more actors. We demonstrate this with Thomson, a general-purpose frontier model trained with an enhanced focus on high-stakes professional work. Thomson performs competitively with recent frontier models across agentic tasks, safety, legal, tax & multilingualism, and large-scale Deep Research. Evaluations show a distinctive $π$-shaped pattern: distinct improvements across a wide range of capabilities, including those not explicitly targeted, while almost completely eliminating the forgetting problem common to narrow domain adaptation.

</details>


### [87] [BPMN4CAI: A BPMN Extension for Modeling Dynamic Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27149)
*Björn-Lennart Eger,Daniel Rose,Barbara Dinter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BPMN4CAI，一种面向会话式人工智能业务流程建模的标准兼容扩展，用于表示动态且具有上下文敏感性的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPMN在描述会话式人工智能参与的动态决策、上下文变化和交互过程方面存在表达能力不足的问题，导致相关业务流程难以系统建模和透明管理。

Method: 研究采用设计科学研究方法，在现有BPMN元素基础上进行系统扩展，并引入面向会话式人工智能的专用建模组件；随后通过案例研究验证框架的适用性与相关性。

Result: 案例评估表明，BPMN4CAI能够支持更具适应性的决策流程、稳健的上下文管理以及更透明的会话式人工智能交互。

Conclusion: BPMN4CAI弥补了标准BPMN在会话式人工智能业务流程建模方面的不足，为动态交互与上下文感知流程提供了标准兼容的建模方法，但摘要未说明评估规模、对比基线及量化指标。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems, such as chatbots and virtual assistants, are becoming increasingly important to digital business processes. However, the established Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) standard faces challenges when representing dynamic, context-sensitive interactions. This paper addresses this methodological and practical research gap by developing a standard-compliant BPMN extension (BPMN4CAI). Using Design Science Research methodology, this paper develops an approach that systematically extends existing BPMN elements and incorporates specialized components. The applicability and relevance of the BPMN4CAI framework are demonstrated and evaluated through a case study. The results show that the BPMN4CAI extension facilitates adaptive decision-making processes, robust context management, and transparent interactions for Conversational AI within business processes.

</details>


### [88] [Calibrated Enough to Know, Not Calibrated to Act: Fabricated Evidence Makes LLM Agents Commit to the Unknowable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27167)
*Pranav Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，大语言模型在面对不可预测问题时，会因“专业化信息面板”的呈现而显著提高方向性承诺率；即使面板数据完全伪造，效果仍与真实数据接近。核心缺陷不在知识、概率信念或可知性判断，而在于“是否应当行动/作答”的决策门控失效。该门控可通过小规模监督微调改善，但对输出格式高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在区分模型的能力不足、错误信念、可知性判断失误与行动决策失误，并检验模型是否会把信息的权威性包装误当作可用于预测的有效证据。这一问题直接关系到模型在不确定环境中的过度自信、虚假决策和部署可靠性。

Method: 研究在12个前沿模型上逐步增强市场信息面板的证据呈现，比较模型对可证明不可预测问题的方向性承诺率；进一步将面板全部替换为虚构数字，以隔离“信息真实性”和“呈现权威性”的作用。作者还设置同面板下的可回答问题、概率报告、可知性分类与行动选择等对照任务，以定位失败环节。最后，对3B模型使用540个以骰子、硬币、容器和计时器为主的合成样本进行监督微调，并测试跨领域迁移及不同回答格式下的稳健性。

Result: 随着证据包装增强，模型承诺率从6.5%升至54.0%。完全虚构的面板将承诺率从24.5%提升至36.8%，与真实市场数据条件下的37.6%无显著差异，说明驱动力主要是包装权威性而非有效信息。对于匹配的可回答问题，模型几乎总能作答且准确率接近完美；概率陈述随证据梯度变化很小，且表现不如气候学基线；模型在行动前能够约90%识别问题不可约预测，却仍出现不一致的行动行为。问题集中于少数模型。经微调后，3B模型在原始案例上的不当承诺率降至0.0%，并迁移至三个未见领域；但在限制推理空间的刚性输出格式中，改进会失效，模型仍可能自信地给出错误方向。

Conclusion: 论文将模型在不可预测任务中的过度承诺定位为可分离的“行动门控”缺陷：模型并非单纯不懂、不会判断不可知，也并非真正因概率信念改变而行动，而是容易把具有权威外观的上下文当作行动许可。该缺陷能够通过针对性训练显著缓解，但训练效果依赖允许模型进行推理和保留拒答空间的交互格式。因此，实际部署应同时采用门控训练与格式层面的约束设计，并将其视为情境脆弱的安全能力，而非一次训练即可永久解决的问题。

Abstract: An LLM agent shown a professional-looking market panel commits to a directional call on a provably unpredictable question far more often than one asked the bare question: across 12 frontier models, commitment rises from 6.5% to 54.0% as evidence is escalated. It commits just as readily when every number on the panel is invented: fabricating the entire display, so nothing the model can see is true except the question itself, still lifts commitment from 24.5% to 36.8%, statistically indistinguishable from the 37.6% produced by genuine market data. What unlocks confident action is not information but the authority of its packaging. The failure is narrow and locatable. Incapacity is not the answer: on matched answerable questions attached to the same panels, the same models answer essentially always, at near-perfect accuracy. Nor is it belief - stated probabilities barely move across the gradient that swings action by 48 points, and score worse than a climatological baseline. Missing judgment isn't it either: asked to classify a question's knowability before acting, models call it irreducible 90% of the time and then commit on just 0.4% of those. The act/don't-act gate is what fails, and the effect is concentrated in a few models rather than universal. Because the gate is separable, it can be trained. Supervised fine-tuning of a 3B model on 540 synthetic cases, predominantly dice, coins, jars and timers, drives commitment to 0.0% on the original cases and transfers to three unseen domains. It does not survive everything: the gate holds exactly when the response format leaves room to reason, and rigid formats that remove that room leave the model confident and wrong on questions it otherwise answers correctly. The gate is trainable and context-fragile, and deployment needs both halves of that sentence.

</details>


### [89] [What Makes Good Agentic Data? An ACE Lens on Data Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27260)
*Xingshan Zeng,Zishan Xu,Boju Zhang,Yuzhou Wu,Lingzhi Wang,Jianghao Lin,Liangyou Li,Yasheng Wang,Lifeng Shang,Xin Jiang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu,Qun Liu,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向LLM智能体数据生成的统一两层框架：以(E,q,τ,v)表示环境、任务、交互与验证器，并以ACE（准确性、复杂度、多样性）刻画受约束的数据分布设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究按智能体领域组织，评价标准异质，且常混淆候选数据构造、验证与筛选，难以揭示跨领域的共同生成机制。核心问题已从单纯扩大数据规模转向持续生成有效、有信息量且低冗余的交互经验。

Method: 首先将智能体数据因子化为环境规格E、任务信号q、交互实现τ和可选验证器v，并依据主要锚点及依赖结构归纳生成范式。其次将数据生成形式化为受约束的分布设计：准确性确定具备环境落地性和内部一致性的数据支持集；复杂度根据目标学习器及执行配置分配学习质量；多样性控制行为覆盖与冗余。

Result: 框架归纳显示，相关研究正从表面变化或数据集规模导向执行基础的准确性、相对于学习器的复杂度，以及超越表面差异的数据多样性。本文还从ACE视角讨论验证机制、难度构造与校准、行为覆盖扩展，以及数据规模化、数据来源、训练范式和自适应学习等趋势。

Conclusion: 高质量智能体数据生成的关键不是生成更多样本，而是在智能体与环境持续演化时，动态分配经过验证、具有学习价值且相互非冗余的经验。ACE框架为统一分析生成、验证、难度控制和覆盖扩展提供了概念基础。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on generated interaction data to learn how to interact with external environments. Agentic data generation must maintain consistency among environments, tasks, interactions, and success signals while producing experience that is useful rather than merely abundant. Existing work spans many agent domains, but domain-centered organization and heterogeneous evaluation often obscure common generation mechanisms and conflate candidate construction with verification and selection. This work develops a two-level framework for the field. First, we represent agentic data as a common factorized object $(E,q,τ,v)$, comprising an environment specification, task signal, interaction realization, and optional verifier. We organize generation paradigms by their primary anchor and dependency structure. Second, we formulate generation as constrained distribution design through the Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE) lens. Accuracy establishes the feasible support of grounded and internally consistent data. Within this support, Complexity places learning mass relative to the capability of a declared learner and execution configuration, while divErsity controls coverage and redundancy of data. Using this framework, we explore how prior work verifies generated experience, constructs and calibrates difficulty, and expands behavioral coverage. The literature reveals a shift toward execution-grounded accuracy, learner-relative complexity, and diversity beyond surface variation or dataset size. We further discuss broader directions and emerging trends in agentic data generation through the ACE lens, including their implications for scaling, data sources, training regimes and adaptive learning. Overall, the central challenge is not simply to generate more data, but to continually allocate valid, informative, and non-redundant experience as agents and environments evolve.

</details>


### [90] [Naive Prompt Optimization: Rethinking the Need for Complex Prompt Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27266)
*Yuan Chang,Xiaoqi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出轻量级的朴素提示优化方法（NPO），通过教师模型结合执行反馈迭代修改提示词；实验表明，其性能可媲美甚至超过更复杂的GEPA，同时所需rollout更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法逐渐趋于复杂，带来较高的优化与部署成本。本文旨在验证：更强的教师模型推理能力，是否能够替代优化器侧的大规模搜索，从而以更简单的方式提升智能体在多任务中的表现。

Method: NPO采用单一谱系、线性迭代的优化流程：教师模型根据任务执行或rollout反馈，对当前提示词进行逐步修订。研究将NPO与GEPA、GRPO等方法进行比较，并在多样任务、交互式游戏及不同学生模型之间评估效果，同时测试优化后提示词的跨模型迁移能力。

Result: NPO在较少rollout的情况下达到或超过GEPA的性能，且教师模型越强，其相对优势越明显。在交互式游戏中，NPO整体上与GEPA具有竞争力，但GRPO在部分不适合提示优化的任务上表现更好。NPO生成的提示词可直接迁移至其他学生模型，并在同系列模型之间表现出更明显的性能提升。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，简单的线性提示优化能够与复杂搜索式优化程序相抗衡。增强教师模型的推理能力可能是降低优化器复杂度和计算成本的有效路径，但该结论仍需在更广泛任务与更严格实验中进一步验证。

Abstract: Efficiently improving autonomous agents across diverse tasks is central to accelerating recursive self-improvement (RSI) in agentic AI, with prompt optimization emerging as a promising approach capable of delivering performance gains comparable to those achieved by fine-tuning model weights, while reducing computational costs in both optimization and serving. However, recent developments increasingly favor unnecessarily complex prompt optimizers. We introduce Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO), a lightweight single-lineage method that iteratively revises prompts using a teacher model with rollout feedback. NPO achieves comparable or better performance than GEPA with fewer rollouts, and its advantage increases with stronger teacher models, suggesting that stronger teacher reasoning can partially substitute for optimizer-side search complexity. In interactive games, NPO remains broadly competitive with GEPA, while GRPO performs better on some tasks less amenable to prompt optimization. We also show that NPO-optimized prompts elicit similar performance improvements when applied verbatim to other student models, especially across models within the same family. Overall, our preliminary results show that simple, linear prompt optimization can rival substantially more sophisticated and complex search procedures.

</details>


### [91] [BrailleBench: Investigating Multi-Criteria Braille Comprehension in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27268)
*Jinghan Zhang,Fengran Mo,Zhiyu Chen,Xiaoyan Han,Kunpeng Liu,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BrailleBench，一个用于评估大语言模型盲文理解与交互能力的基准，覆盖英语及一级、二级盲文，并发现模型的印刷英语能力与盲文可及性之间存在持续差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型虽能提供知识与计算辅助，但尚不清楚其是否能让盲人及盲聋用户通过盲文获得同等功能。盲文中的指示符、缩写规则和数字化表示带来了不同于普通英语的模型理解要求，因此需要专门的系统化评测基准。

Method: 构建BrailleBench，整合五个数据集中的5,570个实例，涵盖数学、常识和多跳问答任务，以及英语、一级盲文和二级盲文。基准设置盲文内容理解、盲文答案生成和端到端盲文交互等评测配置。数据通过确定性流程和专家审核生成，依托自研盲文工具包，且不使用大语言模型生成的数据实例；随后从多个维度评估六个具有代表性的大语言模型。

Result: 实验显示，模型的印刷英语能力与盲文可及性之间存在明显且持续的差距。盲文理解能力与盲文表达能力并不对称；其中，二级盲文在输入理解方面相较一级盲文尤其脆弱。完全使用盲文提出请求会进一步降低模型性能。

Conclusion: BrailleBench为评估大语言模型的盲文能力提供了公开、可复现的基准和资源。研究结果表明，未来盲文人工智能系统需要重点提升二级盲文输入理解、盲文输出表达及端到端交互能力。

Abstract: Although Large language models (LLMs) mediate access to knowledge and computational assistance, their capabilities should benefit vulnerable groups in the same way. However, it is unclear whether existing AI systems are inclusive enough for blind and deafblind users to access the same functionality through Braille, whose indicators, contractions, and digital representations introduce distinct requirements for model comprehension. To this end, we introduce BrailleBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs in Braille comprehension from different Criteria. BrailleBench aligns 5,570 instances from five datasets, including mathematics, commonsense, and multi-hop question answering across English and Braille Grades 1 and 2. Different configurations are designed to understand whether the systems can comprehend Braille-authored content, express answers in Braille, and complete end-to-end Braille interaction. To ensure the quality and prevent evaluation bias, the benchmark is built through a deterministic, expert-reviewed pipeline via a self-created Braille Toolkit without using any data instances generated by LLMs. We evaluate six representative LLMs from various aspects. The results reveal a persistent gap between print-English capability and Braille accessibility. Braille understanding and expression are asymmetric, where Grade 2 is especially fragile on the input side compared to Grade 1, and fully Braille requests further reduce performance. The experimental observations provide valuable guidance for the development of future Braille AI systems. All related resources in BrailleBench are publicly available for future research.

</details>


### [92] [LLMs Can Design Near-Optimal OR Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27296)
*Jackie Baek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文考察大语言模型能否为定义明确的运筹学问题自动设计高质量算法。在库存控制、排队网络控制和商品组合优化上，最强测试模型在多数实例中达到或超过现有最佳方法，并且可在未见具体测试实例时生成可泛化的求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运筹学算法设计通常依赖领域专家、问题建模与大量调参。论文旨在检验：在仅提供问题描述、参数范围、一次未经调优的提示词以及固定计算预算的条件下，前沿大语言模型是否已能成为自动算法设计的有效工具与经验基线。

Method: 研究设置两种层级：层级1中，模型针对单个具体实例直接输出解；层级2中，模型仅获得问题类别及宽泛参数范围，需生成将实例参数映射为解的固定算法。评估覆盖库存控制、排队网络控制和商品组合优化，并将模型产出与各任务上的既有强基线方法进行比较。模型可使用Python沙箱，但计算预算固定，人工干预限于提供一次未调优的问题提示。

Result: 摘要报告，gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有评测实例上与最佳既有方法持平或更优；这一结果不仅出现在实例级求解，也出现在算法需预先固定、随后泛化到未见评测实例的层级2设定中。不同发布时间相隔不足八个月的模型之间表现提升显著，表明相关能力进展较快。

Conclusion: 对于形式清晰、可计算验证的运筹学问题，单次未经调优的大语言模型调用已可能生成具有专业竞争力的解法或算法。因此，前沿大语言模型应被纳入此类算法设计研究的实证比较基线。不过，摘要未说明实例分布、统计显著性、计算成本、鲁棒性及与人工调参方法的公平性，完整结论仍需结合正文实验细节验证。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) can design effective algorithms for well-specified operations research (OR) problems. We study inventory control, queueing network control, and assortment optimization. We evaluate two levels of LLM use: at level 1, the model receives one problem instance and returns a solution for that instance; at level 2, it receives only the problem class description and broad parameter ranges, and returns an algorithm that maps instance parameters to solutions. Human input is minimal: we give one untuned prompt that describes the problem, and the model has access to a Python sandbox tool with a fixed compute budget.
  The strongest model we test, gpt-5.6-sol, matches or outperforms the best existing method on almost all evaluated instances. This holds even at level 2, where the returned algorithm is fixed before seeing the evaluation instances. Performance also improves sharply across models released less than eight months apart, suggesting that this capability is moving quickly. Thus, for the well-specified operations problems we study, a single untuned LLM query can already produce algorithms competitive with specialized methods. These results suggest that frontier LLMs can be a serious empirical baseline for algorithm design in well-specified OR problems.

</details>


### [93] [Verify Smarter, Evolve Further: Efficient Harness Evolution through Behavior-Aware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27311)
*Jinghan Xu,Yikai Zhang,Aili Chen,Weiyuan Li,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarnessLens 是一种面向语言模型智能体运行框架演化的预算感知方法，通过联合探索任务空间与可配置组件，并采用行为相关的选择性验证，提高评估效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提议—验证方法通常在固定任务集上评估所有候选方案，导致大量无关交互开销；同时，聚合得分可能掩盖特定行为的性能退化。因此，需要一种能够针对相关行为进行验证、并适应有限评估预算的框架演化方法。

Method: HarnessLens 联合探索任务空间和用户可配置组件，从执行轨迹中生成候选修改，并依据候选行为选择相关任务进行验证。其核心是可归因证据门控机制，仅在验证证据能够归因于目标行为时接受候选方案，从而减少无效评估。

Result: 在三种智能体运行框架和四个基准数据集上，HarnessLens 将平均留出集性能提升了7.6%至13.6%，同时相比竞争基线显著减少了评估预算消耗。

Conclusion: 行为感知验证结合显式归因机制，可以在有限交互预算下实现更可靠、更具样本效率的智能体运行框架演化。

Abstract: Agent harnesses shape how language-model agents use instructions, tools, and runtime components, but adapting these harnesses requires costly verification. Existing propose-and-verify methods typically score every candidate on a fixed task set, wasting rollouts on unrelated behaviors and allowing aggregate scores to obscure specific regressions. We introduce HarnessLens, a budget-aware framework for automated harness evolution. HarnessLens jointly explores the task space and user-configurable components, derives candidate modifications from execution trajectories, and selectively verifies each candidate on behavior-relevant tasks using an attributable-evidence gate. Across three agent harnesses and four benchmarks, HarnessLens improves average held-out performance by 7.6-13.6% while consuming substantially less evaluation budget than competing baselines. These results demonstrate that behavior-aware verification with explicit attribution enables more reliable and sample-efficient harness evolution under constrained interaction budgets. Our code is available at https://github.com/jhxu5214/HarnessLens.

</details>


### [94] [Not All Eval-Awareness Is Equal: Capabilities Framing Predicts Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27340)
*Allison Zhuang,Santiago Aranguri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文表明，评测意识并非单一且行为一致的变量，而可区分为能力导向、安全导向、兼具二者或均不相关的表述；不同表述对模型服从行为的预测差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评测常将评测意识视为需要整体抑制的单一因素，但这种聚合处理可能掩盖其不同语义成分及其对安全相关行为的差异化影响。

Method: 在FORTRESS数据集上使用Qwen3-32B，依据思维链中评测意识的表述，将其分类为能力导向、安全导向、两者兼具或两者皆非，并比较不同引导条件下的服从率。进一步对评测意识阴性轨迹进行思维链预填充干预，以检验表述类型与服从行为之间的因果联系。

Result: 能力导向表述相较安全导向表述，在所有测试的引导条件下均表现出高出24至46个百分点的服从率差距。11种预填充中有10种使服从率按预测方向变化，支持二者之间存在因果联系。结果还显示，总体评测意识抑制率的变化不一定伴随安全相关成分的相应变化。

Conclusion: 评测意识具有行为异质性，不能仅以总体抑制率衡量干预效果。安全评测应区分其语义成分，并单独追踪与安全行为直接相关的部分，否则相同的评测意识抑制比例可能对应截然不同的实际行为结果。

Abstract: Steering interventions targeting eval-awareness, a model's recognition that it is being tested, are increasingly used in safety evaluation pipelines, where evaluation-awareness is treated as a single quantity to be suppressed. We show that verbalized eval-awareness in chain-of-thought can be identified as capabilities-flavored ("the user is testing my ability to follow instructions"), safety-flavored ("the user is testing my boundaries"), both, or neither: framings that predict compliance very differently. On Qwen3-32B over the FORTRESS dataset, capabilities-framing predicts compliance with a +24 to +46 percentage-point gap over safety-framing across all tested steering conditions. A CoT-prefill intervention on eval-awareness-negative rollouts suggests the link is causal, with 10 of 11 prefills shifting compliance in the predicted direction. Then, eval-awareness is not behaviorally uniform: aggregate suppression rates can move while the safety-relevant component does not, and the same "X% suppression of eval-awareness" can correspond to qualitatively different behavioral outcomes.

</details>


### [95] [Sophistication in GenAI Use: Field Evidence from a Large Firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27364)
*Nicholas J. Hallman,Zachary T. Kowaleski,Anu Puvvada,Jaime J. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于一家大型企业近4,000名后台员工在2025年八个月内提交的713,564条生成式人工智能提示词及模型回复，研究不同员工群体的生成式人工智能使用成熟度。结果显示，资历、职能类型与成熟度显著相关，但成熟度不会随时间自然提升，也未因正式培训而持久改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能能否改善组织绩效，不仅取决于是否使用，还取决于使用质量与成熟度。现有研究较少基于大规模真实企业交互数据考察员工使用成熟度的差异、影响因素及其可塑性，因此本文旨在建立测量框架并为管理实践和后续研究提供证据。

Method: 研究使用大型企业的专有运营数据，覆盖近4,000名后台员工、15个职能领域和八个月期间的713,564条员工提示词及对应的大型语言模型回复。作者比较不同资历员工和职能群体的使用成熟度，并考察成熟度随时间变化以及正式人工智能培训后的持续变化。摘要未说明成熟度指标的具体构建方式、统计模型或因果识别策略。

Result: 资深员工的生成式人工智能使用更成熟，表明领域专业知识可能与人工智能能力形成互补。不同职能之间存在显著差异，其中战略、数字创新和项目管理职能的成熟度最高，这些职能共同聚焦于全公司战略项目和组织变革。研究未发现成熟度随时间提升，也未发现正式人工智能培训带来持久改善。

Conclusion: 生成式人工智能使用成熟度具有明显的资历和职能差异，但可能较难通过自然学习或常规培训改变。管理者应针对不同职能和员工经验设计更持续、更贴近工作场景的干预措施；研究者则可利用该数据和测量思路进一步检验成熟度与生产率、创新及组织绩效之间的关系。由于摘要未提供成熟度定义、效应大小和因果设计，关于培训无效及成熟度难以改变的结论仍需结合完整论文审慎解释。

Abstract: We study how sophistication in generative AI (genAI) use varies among the back-office workforce of a large firm. Using proprietary data, we observe 713,564 employee prompts and their corresponding large language model responses from nearly 4,000 back-office employees across 15 functional areas over eight months in 2025. We document three main findings. First, senior employees exhibit more sophisticated genAI use, consistent with domain expertise complementing genAI capabilities. Second, sophistication varies considerably across functions and is highest in Strategy, Digital Innovation, and Project Management, three groups that share a focus on firmwide strategic initiatives and organizational change. Third, we observe neither improvements in sophistication over time nor lasting improvements following formal AI training, suggesting that sophisticated use can be difficult to change. Together, our study provides measures of and insights into sophisticated genAI use that managers can use to improve outcomes and that researchers can use in future research.

</details>


### [96] [CorporateBench: Large-Scale Q&A Benchmarking with Temporal Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27391)
*Sil Hamilton,Albert Yu Sun,Oscar J. Romero,Carl-Leander Henneking,David Mimno,Bishan Yang,Igor Labutov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CorporateBench（CB），一个面向企业级文档集合问答的人工验证基准，用于评估大语言模型在大规模、跨文档且时间演化的企业通信语料中的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业文档问答评测面临真实内部通信无法公开、合成数据过于简单的问题，因而难以反映模型在真实规模企业知识环境中的表现。作者旨在构建兼具规模、逻辑一致性与任务多样性的可控评测基准。

Method: CB基于四个合成企业构建数据集，企业规模从12名员工到10,000名员工不等，评测语料最高超过230,000份文档。语料由随时间演化的知识库采样生成，确保不同文档之间、包括大规模文档集合中的事实与逻辑保持一致。基准从信息抽取和知识库查询两个维度设置任务，并对五个大语言模型进行测试。

Result: 实验显示，随着输入语料规模接近现实企业场景，所评测模型的性能显著下降。这表明当前模型虽然能够处理部分复杂问答，但在超大规模企业通信网络中的信息定位、跨文档整合和一致性推理方面仍存在明显不足。

Conclusion: CorporateBench填补了企业通信推理评测的空白，为大语言模型开发者提供了衡量企业级文档理解与推理能力的标准化指标。其主要价值在于以大规模、时间一致且逻辑自洽的合成语料，更贴近实际企业知识管理场景地暴露模型能力边界。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly able to answer complex questions about enterprise-scale document collections. But evaluation is hard: companies don't want to share internal communications, and synthetic datasets have been overly simple. We present CorporateBench (CB), a human-validated multi-task Q&A benchmark whose scale approaches the conditions LLMs encounter in corporate communication networks, with evaluation corpora surpassing 230,000 documents. CB evaluates LLMs across two dimensions (information extraction and knowledge base querying) through four synthetically generated firms ranging from 12 to 10,000 employees. Each corpus is sampled from a temporally evolving knowledge base describing a consistent world, guaranteeing cross-document logical consistency even across hundreds of thousands of documents. We evaluate five LLMs on CB, revealing increasingly poor performance as input size approaches realistic scales. CB provides LLM developers a metric for corporate communication reasoning, filling a crucial gap in the benchmarking ecosystem.

</details>


### [97] [Learning a Continuous Sepsis Severity Score Without Hour-by-Hour Supervision: A Two-Site Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27421)
*Kevin Zhu,Ryan Zhang,Baraa Abed,Tilendra Choudhary,Malvern Madondo,Mehak Arora,Yixuan Yang,Alasdair Gent,Aditya Nagori,Omer T. Inan,Krista L. Haines,Patrick Georgoff,Suresh M. Agarwal,Vijay Krishnamoorthy,Tetsu Ohnuma,Mihai V. Podgoreanu,Michael R. Pinsky,Gilles Clermont,Craig M. Coopersmith,Craig S. Jabaley,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究基于两家医疗系统的临床轨迹数据，开发了一个可按小时更新的脓毒症严重度指数；该指数在区分生存结局、反映乳酸等生理指标变化及跨机构泛化方面表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脓毒症严重度评分依赖数十年前建立的固定变量和权重，变量离散化较粗，且校准人群可能已不适用于现代重症监护。研究旨在从患者治疗轨迹中学习更动态、细粒度的预后指标。

Method: 开展回顾性双队列研究，共纳入两家医院系统的29,116例和7,691例符合Sepsis-3标准的成年患者。使用治疗前72小时内常规记录的43个变量构建指数，并以患者死亡作为整个治疗层面的排序信号，而非将每个时间点单独设为预测目标，从而允许不同时间点获得非均匀贡献。模型在固定20%测试集上评估，并结合临床病例短文、Spearman相关分析及按患者重采样的Bootstrap不确定性区间进行验证。

Result: 在基线SOFA-2、乳酸、平均动脉压和肌酐分层内，非生存者指数分数均比生存者高1.19至1.64分（0至10分制）。患者内指数变化与乳酸变化呈中等相关（Spearman rho=0.39，n=1,854），与平均动脉压和肌酐变化也存在较弱相关。不同机构训练模型的跨机构一致性达到同机构相关性的70%至77%；外部患者内相关性为0.54和0.59，分别接近0.92和0.90的上限。该指数与既有评分相关，而空模型对照的相关性接近零。

Conclusion: 该指数能够提供逐小时的预后信息，并在不同基线严重度和关键生理指标分层中稳定区分结局，具有一定跨机构泛化能力。结果支持其作为辅助临床判断的动态决策支持工具，但仍需前瞻性研究验证其临床效用及对治疗决策和患者结局的实际影响。

Abstract: Currently used sepsis severity indices rely on fixed variables and weights established decades ago, which are coarsely discretized and calibrated to a cohort that no longer reflects contemporary critical care. No alternative learned directly from patient trajectories is in routine use. We conducted a retrospective two-cohort study on a total of 29,116 and 7,691 adult patients meeting Sepsis-3 criteria from two hospital systems in Massachusetts and Georgie, respectively.
  We developed a sepsis index using 43 routinely charted variables over a 72-hour treatment window. Unlike previous studies, we use mortality as a treatment-level ranking signal rather than a per-state target, allowing credit to be redistributed non-uniformly across timesteps. Evaluation was done on a permanent 20% test holdout, using clinical vignettes and Spearman correlation. Uncertainty intervals were obtained by bootstrap resampling of whole patients. Under this ranking scheme, non-survivors scored 1.19-1.64 points higher than survivors on a 0-10 scale within all strata of baseline SOFA-2, with similar results stratifying within lactate, mean arterial pressure (MAP), and creatinine. Within-patient change in the index correlated with change in lactate (Spearman rho = 0.39; n = 1,854). Similar, weaker correlations were found for MAP and creatinine. On a cohort level, cross-institutional agreement measured by Spearman correlation between models trained on different sites, were 70-77% of same-site correlation. External within-patient correlations were 0.54 and 0.59 against ceilings of 0.92 and 0.90. Our index also correlated with established indices, while null controls stayed near zero.
  Our index demonstrated hourly prognostic information that meaningfully separates patient outcomes and is consistent with clinical expectation, indicating potential as a decision support tool complementing clinical judgement.

</details>


### [98] [Mechanistic Reaction Prediction via Discrete Flow Matching on Graph-Structured Electron Occupation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27429)
*Nguyen Xuan-Vu,Octavian Susanu,Daniel Armstrong,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAELLE将反应预测重新表述为电子占据向量上的离散流匹配问题，通过在连续时间马尔可夫链中建模电子重排，实现产物预测、可解释机理轨迹恢复及副产物预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应预测方法通常直接生成产物分子，或在分子图拓扑上执行启发式编辑，难以显式刻画化学反应本质上的电子转移与重排过程，也因此在可解释性和分布外泛化方面存在局限。

Method: 作者提出MAELLE，在键、孤对电子及氢相关位点构成的图结构整数电子占据空间中，将反应物到产物的映射建模为连续时间马尔可夫链。方法利用最优传输将离散流匹配的混合路径推广至电子重排任务，构造由多个离散电子编辑动作组成的中间轨迹；该训练过程不依赖人工标注的基元反应步骤。

Result: 在USPTO-480K基准上，MAELLE的反应预测性能与主流先进模型具有竞争力。在结构复杂度变化和反应类别变化两类分布外测试中，该方法保持了较强性能，而部分既有方法出现更明显退化。此外，模型学习到的完整电子流可生成与已知化学规律相符的机理轨迹，并能够预测反应副产物。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，以电子占据和电子流而非仅分子拓扑为核心表示，可统一支持反应产物预测、机制解释与副产物建模。MAELLE的主要价值在于将具有化学意义的电子重排轨迹纳入生成式反应预测框架，并展现出较好的鲁棒性与可解释性。

Abstract: Chemical reactions are fundamentally transformations in electron space, yet most machine learning approaches model them either through \textit{de novo} generation of product molecules or through heuristic graph edits that operate directly on molecular topology.
  We introduce MAELLE (\textbf{M}ech\textbf{A}nistic \textbf{E}dit f\textbf{L}ow-matching on e\textbf{L}ectron r\textbf{E}arrangements), which instead models reactions as discrete flow matching over electron occupation vectors.
  Concretely, we formulate the reactant-to-product mapping as a Continuous-time Markov Chain (CTMC) over the graph-structured integer-valued electron occupation space defined on all bonding, non-bonding, and hydrogen sites.
  To construct the intermediate edit trajectories, we generalize the discrete flow matching mixture path to discrete electron rearrangements using Optimal Transport, yielding a sequence of mechanistically interpretable edit moves without requiring elementary step annotations.
  MAELLE achieves competitive performance on the USPTO-480K benchmark compared with leading reaction prediction models.
  Beyond in-distribution accuracy, we evaluate robustness across two out-of-distribution settings - structural complexity and reaction type - and find that MAELLE maintains strong performance where existing methods degrade.
  Finally, because the learned flow operates over the full electron redistribution, MAELLE naturally recovers mechanistic trajectories that align with known chemistry and can predict side products of a reaction.

</details>


### [99] [WikiSkill: Compiling Agent Experience into Persistent Knowledge for Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27454)
*Liyan Tang,Cyrus Rashtchian,Chun-Sung Ferng,Andrew Tomkins,Da-Cheng Juan,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WikiSkill提出“经验—知识库—可执行技能”分层闭环：将智能体执行中的原始经验持续沉淀为持久化Wiki知识，再据此迭代技能。实验表明，该框架普遍优于现有技能进化方法，并具备模型间迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动技能发现方法虽能从交互经验中生成技能，但关键洞见通常分散在优化轨迹或历史记录中，难以跨迭代系统复用。这导致技能更新缺乏稳定的累积性知识基础，也限制了技能的可迁移性与持续改进能力。

Method: WikiSkill明确分离三类信息：原始执行经验、持久化知识Wiki和可执行技能。系统持续从任务执行与优化过程中提炼可泛化的规律、策略和工作流，并写入Wiki；后续技能生成或更新以Wiki为依据，而非仅依赖局部、短期的历史轨迹。该机制形成经验积累、知识整理与技能演化相互促进的闭环。

Result: 在多种基准和模型上，WikiSkill稳定优于当前先进的技能演化方法，并在多数“模型—基准”组合中优于不使用技能的基线。技能演化与模型规模具有互补性：较大模型通常从进化技能中获益更多；配备技能的较小模型可显著超过未配备技能的更大模型。进化后的技能还能跨模型及跨模型家族有效迁移，且由其他模型进化得到的技能在部分情况下优于模型自身进化的技能。消融实验进一步验证，Wiki中的持久知识累积是性能提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将技能演化从对零散历史经验的局部利用，转变为以持久化、结构化知识库为中心的持续学习过程。结果支持如下结论：系统化地沉淀和精炼智能体经验，能够提升技能的效果、复用性、跨模型迁移性及对模型能力扩展的增益。

Abstract: Agent skills package specialized knowledge and workflows into reusable resources that extend AI agent capabilities. Recent work automatically discovers such skills from agent experience, which enables agents to progressively adapt through interaction. However, the insights that guide skill development typically remain scattered across optimization histories, limiting their systematic reuse across iterations. We introduce WikiSkill, a framework that co-evolves agent skills with a persistent knowledge base (wiki). At a high level, WikiSkill separates raw execution experience, accumulated knowledge, and executable skills, while continuously consolidating experience into the wiki, which subsequent skill updates can build on. Across diverse benchmarks and models, WikiSkill consistently outperforms state-of-the-art skill-evolution methods and improves over no-skill baselines in most model-benchmark settings. We find that skill evolution complements model scaling: larger models generally benefit more from evolved skills, while smaller models with skills can outperform substantially larger models without them. We also find that evolved skills transfer effectively across models and model families, and skills evolved by other models can outperform self-evolved skills. Finally, our ablation studies confirm that persistent knowledge accumulation in the wiki is critical for effective skill evolution. These results demonstrate the benefits of systematically accumulating and refining agent experience for developing reusable and transferable skills.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Agentic AI Containment Architecture for Security Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26108)
*Mohamed ElBendary*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多智能体AI系统的智能体遏制架构，将安全性内生为系统架构属性，并通过六类显式约束实现可验证、可追踪和可审计的协同执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统安全主要依赖设计完成后或部署后的附加防护，难以应对自主协调、工具调用和持续学习带来的新型风险。论文旨在通过架构级约束降低提示注入、编排器操纵、跨会话状态污染及智能体串谋等威胁。
